1173: // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
1174: export const Intro: React.FC = () => {
1175:   const frame = useCurrentFrame();
1176:   const { fps, width, height } = useVideoConfig();
1177:   const ms = (milliseconds: number) => Math.round(milliseconds / (1000 / fps));
1178: 
1179:   // ── 1. The "Camera Zoom Out" effect (Frame 155 to 185) ────────────────────
1180:   const zoomFrame = ms(5200); // Frame 156
1181:   const zoomProgress = interpolate(frame, [zoomFrame, zoomFrame + ms(1000)], [0, 1], {
1182:     easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.cubic),
1183:     extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp',
1184:   });
1185:   
1186:   // Scale down from full screen to phone size
1187:   const phoneScale = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [1.174, 0.72]); // 1.174 scales 920 up to 1080
1188:   const phoneRadius = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [0, 148]);
1189:   const bezelOpacity = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [0, 1]);
1190: 
1191:   // ── 2. Transition Fade (Crossfade Intro -> Dashboard) ─────────────────────
1192:   // Logo & particles fade OUT
1193:   const introOpacity = interpolate(frame, [zoomFrame + ms(800), zoomFrame + ms(1400)], [1, 0], {
1194:     extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp',
1195:   });
1196:   // Dashboard elements stagger IN
1197:   const dashboardRevealProgress = interpolate(frame, [zoomFrame + ms(1200), zoomFrame + ms(2200)], [0, 1], {
1198:     extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp',
1199:   });
1200:                   padding: `${8 * S}px ${20 * S}px`, borderRadius: 8 * S, background: APP_TEXT_COLOR,
1201:                   color: 'black', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: 16 * S, fontFamily: FONT_FLUTTER
1202:                 }}>تم</div>
1203:               </div>
1204:             </div>
1205:           </div>
1206:         )}
1207: 
1208:       </div>
1209:     </AbsoluteFill>
1210:   );
1211: };

1891: export const Intro: React.FC = () => {
1892:   const frame = useCurrentFrame();
1893:   const { fps, width, height } = useVideoConfig();
1894:   const ms = (milliseconds: number) => Math.round(milliseconds / (1000 / fps));
1895: 
1896:   // ── 1. The "Camera Zoom Out" effect (Frame 155 to 185) ────────────────────
1897:   const zoomFrame = ms(5200); // Frame 156
1898:   const zoomProgress = interpolate(frame, [zoomFrame, zoomFrame + ms(1000)], [0, 1], {
1899:     easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.cubic),
1900:     extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp',
1901:   });
1902:   
1903:   // Scale down from full screen to phone size
1904:   const phoneScale = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [1.174, 0.72]); // 1.174 scales 920 up to 1080
1905:   const phoneRadius = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [0, 148]);
1906:   const bezelOpacity = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [0, 1]);
1907: 
1908:   // ── 2. Transition Fade (Crossfade Intro -> Dashboard) ─────────────────────
1909:   // Logo & particles fade OUT
1910:   const introOpacity = interpolate(frame, [zoomFrame + ms(800), zoomFrame + ms(1400)], [1, 0], {
1911:     extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp',
1912:   });
1913:   // Dashboard elements stagger IN
1914:   const dashboardRevealProgress = interpolate(frame, [zoomFrame + ms(1200), zoomFrame + ms(2200)], [0, 1], {
1915:     extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp',
1916:   });
1917: 
1918:   // Flat camera angle - no tilting or floating
1919:   const floatY = 0;
1920:   const tiltX = 0;
1921:   const tiltY = 0;
1922: 
1923:   // ── Constants for Intro ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
1924:   const LOGO_SIZE = 200;
1925:   const FONT_SIZE = 60;
1926:   const LETTER_SPACING = 14;
1927:   const TEXT_FULL_WIDTH = 480; 
1928: 
1929:   // ── Intro Animations ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1930:   const logoOpacity = interpolate(frame, [ms(600), ms(600) + ms(800)], [0, 1], { extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp' });
1931:   const logoScale = interpolate(frame, [ms(600), ms(600) + ms(1500)], [0.88, 1.0], { easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic), extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp' });
1932:   const sweepProgress = interpolate(frame, [ms(1000), ms(1000) + ms(1500)], [-100, 200], { extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp' });
1933: 
1934:   // 3D Floating Tilt for the logo
1935:   const logoTiltX = Math.sin(frame / 30) * 4; 
1936:   const logoTiltY = Math.cos(frame / 40) * 4; 
1937:   const logoFloatY = Math.sin(frame / 20) * 6; 
1938: 
1939:   // Particles
1940:   const particles = new Array(25).fill(0).map((_, i) => {
1941:     const startX = random(`x-${i}`) * width;
1942:     const startY = height + random(`y-${i}`) * 800;
1943:     const speed = 0.5 + random(`speed-${i}`) * 1.5;
1944:     const size = 1 + random(`size-${i}`) * 3;
1945:     const baseOpacity = 0.1 + random(`op-${i}`) * 0.4;
1946:     return { x: startX + Math.sin((frame + i * 10) / 40) * 30, y: startY - (frame * speed), size, opacity: baseOpacity };
1947:   });
1948: 
1949:   // Text emerge
1950:   const emergeStart = ms(1400);
2950:               <div
2951:                 style={{
2952:                   width: "100%",
2953:                   display: "flex",
2954:                   justifyContent: "flex-end",
2955:                   marginTop: 16 * S,
2956:                 }}
2957:               >
2958:                 <div
2959:                   style={{
2960:                     padding: `${8 * S}px ${20 * S}px`,
2961:                     borderRadius: 8 * S,
2962:                     background: APP_TEXT_COLOR,
2963:                     color: "black",
2964:                     fontWeight: "bold",
2965:                     fontSize: 16 * S,
2966:                     fontFamily: FONT_FLUTTER,
2967:                   }}
2968:                 >
2969:                   تم
2970:                 </div>
2971:               </div>
2972:             </div>
2973:           </div>
2974:         )}
2975:       </div>
2976:     </AbsoluteFill>
2977:   );
2978: };

2983: 
2984: export const Intro: React.FC = () => {
2985:   const frame = useCurrentFrame();
2986:   const { fps, width, height } = useVideoConfig();
2987:   const ms = (milliseconds: number) => Math.round(milliseconds / (1000 / fps));
2988: 
2989:   // ── 1. The "Camera Zoom Out" effect (Frame 155 to 185) ────────────────────
2990:   const zoomFrame = ms(5200); // Frame 156
2991:   const zoomProgress = interpolate(
2992:     frame,
2993:     [zoomFrame, zoomFrame + ms(1000)],
2994:     [0, 1],
2995:     {
2996:       easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.cubic),
2997:       extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
2998:       extrapolateRight: "clamp",
2999:     },
3000:   );
3001: 
3002:   // Scale down from full screen to phone size
3003:   const phoneScale = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [1.174, 0.72]); // 1.174 scales 920 up to 1080
3004:   const phoneRadius = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [0, 148]);
3005:   const bezelOpacity = interpolate(zoomProgress, [0, 1], [0, 1]);
3006: 
3007:   // ── 2. Transition Fade (Crossfade Intro -> Dashboard) ─────────────────────
3008:   // Logo & particles fade OUT
3009:   const introOpacity = interpolate(
3010:     frame,
3011:     [zoomFrame + ms(800), zoomFrame + ms(1400)],
3012:     [1, 0],
3013:     {
3014:       extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
3015:       extrapolateRight: "clamp",
3016:     },
3017:   );
3018:   // Dashboard elements stagger IN
3019:   const dashboardRevealProgress = interpolate(
3020:     frame,
3021:     [zoomFrame + ms(1200), zoomFrame + ms(2200)],
3022:     [0, 1],
3023:     {
3024:       extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
3025:       extrapolateRight: "clamp",
3026:     },
3027:   );
3028: 
3029:   // Flat camera angle - no tilting or floating
3030:   const floatY = 0;
3031:   const tiltX = 0;
3032:   const tiltY = 0;
3033: 
3034:   // ── Constants for Intro ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
3035:   const LOGO_SIZE = 200;
3036:   const FONT_SIZE = 60;
3037:   const LETTER_SPACING = 14;
3038:   const TEXT_FULL_WIDTH = 480;
3039: 
3040:   // ── Intro Animations ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
3041:   const logoOpacity = interpolate(frame, [ms(600), ms(600) + ms(800)], [0, 1], {
3042:     extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
3043:     extrapolateRight: "clamp",
3044:   });
3045:   const logoScale = interpolate(
3046:     frame,
3047:     [ms(600), ms(600) + ms(1500)],
3048:     [0.88, 1.0],
3049:     {
3050:       easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
3051:       extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
3052:       extrapolateRight: "clamp",
3053:     },
3054:   );
3055:   const sweepProgress = interpolate(
3056:     frame,
3057:     [ms(1000), ms(1000) + ms(1500)],
3058:     [-100, 200],
3059:     { extrapolateLeft: "clamp", extrapolateRight: "clamp" },
3060:   );
3061: 
3062:   // 3D Floating Tilt for the logo
3063:   const logoTiltX = Math.sin(frame / 30) * 4;
3064:   const logoTiltY = Math.cos(frame / 40) * 4;
3065:   const logoFloatY = Math.sin(frame / 20) * 6;
3066: 
3067:   // Particles
3068:   const particles = new Array(25).fill(0).map((_, i) => {
3069:     const startX = random(`x-${i}`) * width;
3070:     const startY = height + random(`y-${i}`) * 800;
3071:     const speed = 0.5 + random(`speed-${i}`) * 1.5;
3072:     const size = 1 + random(`size-${i}`) * 3;
3073:     const baseOpacity = 0.1 + random(`op-${i}`) * 0.4;
3074:     return {
3075:       x: startX + Math.sin((frame + i * 10) / 40) * 30,
3076:       y: startY - frame * speed,
3077:       size,
3078:       opacity: baseOpacity,
3079:     };
3080:   });
3081: 
3082:   // Text emerge
3083:   const emergeStart = ms(1400);
3084:   const emergeDur = ms(1200);
3085:   const emergeProgress = interpolate(
3086:     frame,
3087:     [emergeStart, emergeStart + emergeDur],
3088:     [0, 1],
3089:     {
3090:       easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
3091:       extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
3092:       extrapolateRight: "clamp",
3093:     },
3094:   );
3095:   const textWidth = emergeProgress * TEXT_FULL_WIDTH;
3096:   const calliOpacity = interpolate(
3097:     frame,
3098:     [emergeStart, emergeStart + ms(600)],
3099:     [0, 1],
3100:     { extrapolateLeft: "clamp", extrapolateRight: "clamp" },
3101:   );
3102: 
3103:   // GRO wave
3104:   const groLetters = ["G", "R", "O"].map((letter, idx) => {
3105:     const fadeStart = emergeStart + ms(idx * 100);
3106:     const opacity = interpolate(
3107:       frame,
3108:       [fadeStart, fadeStart + ms(600)],
3109:       [0, 1],
3110:       { extrapolateLeft: "clamp", extrapolateRight: "clamp" },
3111:     );
3112:     const waveDelay = ms(2800 + idx * 150);
3113:     const waveDur = ms(500);
3114:     let moveY = 0;
3115:     if (frame >= waveDelay && frame < waveDelay + waveDur) {
3116:       moveY =
3117:         -22 *
3118:         interpolate(frame, [waveDelay, waveDelay + waveDur], [0, 1], {
3119:           easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.cubic),
3120:         });
3121:     } else if (
3122:       frame >= waveDelay + waveDur &&
3123:       frame < waveDelay + waveDur * 2
3124:     ) {
3125:       moveY =
3126:         -22 +
3127:         44 *
3128:           interpolate(
3129:             frame,
3130:             [waveDelay + waveDur, waveDelay + waveDur * 2],
3131:             [0, 1],
3132:             { easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.cubic) },
3133:           );
3134:     } else if (
3135:       frame >= waveDelay + waveDur * 2 &&
3136:       frame < waveDelay + waveDur * 3
3137:     ) {
3138:       moveY =
3139:         22 *
3140:         (1 -
3141:           interpolate(
3142:             frame,
3143:             [waveDelay + waveDur * 2, waveDelay + waveDur * 3],
3144:             [0, 1],
3145:             { easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.cubic) },
3146:           ));
3147:     }
3148:     return { letter, opacity, moveY };
3149:   });
3150: 
3151:   // Text retract
3152:   const retractStart = ms(3800);
3153:   const retractDur = ms(1000);
3154:   const retractProgress = interpolate(
3155:     frame,
3156:     [retractStart, retractStart + retractDur],
3157:     [0, 1],
3158:     {
3159:       easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.cubic),
3160:       extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
3161:       extrapolateRight: "clamp",
3162:     },
3163:   );
3164:   const containerWidth =
3165:     frame >= retractStart ? (1 - retractProgress) * TEXT_FULL_WIDTH : textWidth;
3166:   const showText =
3167:     frame >= emergeStart &&
3168:     (frame >= retractStart ? retractProgress < 1 : true);
3169: 
3170:   // Pulse & Shockwave
3171:   const pulseFrame = retractStart + retractDur;
3172:   const pulseActive = frame >= pulseFrame && frame < pulseFrame + ms(500);
3173:   const pulseSpring = pulseActive
3174:     ? spring({
3175:         frame: frame - pulseFrame,
3176:         fps,
3177:         config: { damping: 7, stiffness: 220, mass: 0.3 },
3178:       })
3179:     : 0;
3180:   const shockwaveProgress = interpolate(
3181:     frame,
3182:     [pulseFrame, pulseFrame + ms(1000)],
3183:     [0, 1],
3184:     { extrapolateLeft: "clamp", extrapolateRight: "clamp" },
3185:   );
3186: 
3187:   // Gold lines
3188:   const lineStart = ms(200);
3189:   const lineScaleY = interpolate(
3190:     frame,
3191:     [lineStart, lineStart + ms(400)],
3192:     [0, 1],
3193:     {
3194:       easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
3195:       extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
3196:       extrapolateRight: "clamp",
3197:     },
3198:   );
3199:   const lineMoveX = interpolate(
3200:     frame,
3201:     [lineStart + ms(400), lineStart + ms(1200)],
3202:     [0, 300],
3203:     {
3204:       easing: Easing.out(Easing.poly(4)),
3205:       extrapolateLeft: "clamp",
3206:       extrapolateRight: "clamp",
3207:     },
3208:   );
3209:   const lineFade = interpolate(
3210:     frame,
3211:     [lineStart + ms(400), lineStart + ms(800)],
3212:     [1, 0],
3213:     { extrapolateLeft: "clamp", extrapolateRight: "clamp" },
3214:   );
3215: 
3216:   // Shimmer
3217:   const shimmerStart = ms(2600);
3218:   const shimmerDur = ms(1500);
3219:   const shimmerPos = interpolate(
3220:     frame,
3221:     [shimmerStart, shimmerStart + shimmerDur],
3222:     [-20, 120],
3223:     { extrapolateLeft: "clamp", extrapolateRight: "clamp" },
